#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

// Compute Laplacian with clamped boundary conditions
double computeLaplacian(const std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double dx, const double dy, const double dz, const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z) {
    const size_t xp = (x < nx - 1) ? x + 1 : x;
    const size_t yp = (y < ny - 1) ? y + 1 : y;
    const size_t zp = (z < nz - 1) ? z + 1 : z;
    const size_t xn = (x > 0) ? x - 1 : 0;
    const size_t yn = (y > 0) ? y - 1 : 0;
    const size_t zn = (z > 0) ? z - 1 : 0;
    
    const double cxx = (c[idx3(xp, y, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(xn, y, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dx * dx);
    const double cyy = (c[idx3(x, yp, z, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, yn, z, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dy * dy);
    const double czz = (c[idx3(x, y, zp, nx, ny)] + c[idx3(x, y, zn, nx, ny)] - 
                  2.0 * c[idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny)]) / (dz * dz);
    
    return cxx + cyy + czz;
}

// Compute chemical potential
void computeChemicalPotential(const std::vector<double>& c, std::vector<double>& mu,
                              const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                              const double dx, const double dy, const double dz,
                              const double gamma, const double e_AA, const double e_BB, const double e_AB) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                const double cv = c[idx];
                
                mu[idx] = 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB) 
                         + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv
                         - gamma * computeLaplacian(c, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Cahn-Hilliard update step
void cahnHilliardUpdate(std::vector<double>& cnew, const std::vector<double>& cold,
                        const std::vector<double>& mu,
                        const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                        const double D, const double dt, const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                cnew[idx] = cold[idx] + dt * D * 
                           computeLaplacian(mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, x, y, z);
            }
        }
    }
}

// Initialize concentration field
void initializeConcentration(std::vector<double>& c, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    const size_t vol = nx * ny * nz;
    
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                // Generate pseudo-random value in [-1, 1]
                const size_t linear_id = z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
                const double pseudo = ((((linear_id + 1) * 1299709) % vol) / static_cast<double>(vol));
                c[idx] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Physical parameters
    const double dx = 1.0;
    const double dy = 1.0;
    const double dz = 1.0;
    const double dt = 0.01;
    const double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    const double e_AB = (2.0 / 9.0);
    const double gamma = 0.5;
    const double D = 1.0;
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate arrays
    std::vector<double> cold(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> cnew(gridSize);
    std::vector<double> mu(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize concentration field
    printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
    initializeConcentration(cold, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int t = 0; t < iterations; ++t) {
        // Compute chemical potential
        computeChemicalPotential(cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, dx, dy, dz, 
                                gamma, e_AA, e_BB, e_AB);
        
        // Update concentration
        cahnHilliardUpdate(cnew, cold, mu, nx, ny, nz, D, dt, dx, dy, dz);
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(cold, cnew);
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance
    double cellUpdates = (double)gridSize * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status "'!= cudaSuccess) {

    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Each warp follows X, so all seven stencil loads are coalesced. Kernel
// boundaries provide the global synchronization between the two stencils.
template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned z = blockIdx.z; z < nz; z += gridDim.z) {
        for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + y * nx + x;
            const double center = input[i];
            const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                               input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                               input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double czz = input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)] +
                               input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
            } else {
                output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

'"''' + s[b:]

        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\\\\n\");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, nz)));

        printf(\"Initializing concentration field...\\\\n\");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf(\"Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\\\\n\");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\\\n\", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf(\"Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\\\\n\", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA simulation failed: %s\\\\n\", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

#include <algorithm>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Each warp follows X, so all seven stencil loads are coalesced. Kernel
// boundaries provide the global synchronization between the two stencils.
template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned z = blockIdx.z; z < nz; z += gridDim.z) {
        for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + y * nx + x;
            const double center = input[i];
            const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                               input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                               input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double czz = input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)] +
                               input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
            } else {
                output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Each warp follows X, so all seven stencil loads are coalesced. Kernel
// boundaries provide the global synchronization between the two stencils.
template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned z = blockIdx.z; z < nz; z += gridDim.z) {
        for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(z) * plane + y * nx + x;
            const double center = input[i];
            const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                               input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                               input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double czz = input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)] +
                               input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
            } else {
                output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, nz)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

// Each warp follows X, so all seven stencil loads are coalesced. Kernel
// boundaries provide the global synchronization between the two stencils.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            size_t i = zBase * plane + y * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            const unsigned count = static_cast<unsigned>(min(size_t{ZTile}, size_t{nz} - zBase));
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                                   input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                                   input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
            } else {
                output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
            }
            previous = center;
            center = following;
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Each warp follows X, so all seven stencil loads are coalesced. Kernel
// boundaries provide the global synchronization between the two stencils.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t y = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            size_t i = zBase * plane + y * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            const unsigned count = static_cast<unsigned>(min(size_t{ZTile}, size_t{nz} - zBase));
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                                   input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                                   input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
            const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
            } else {
                output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
            }
            previous = center;
            center = following;
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + ZTile - 1) / ZTile)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
a=s.index('// Each warp follows X')
b=s.index('bool validateResult',a)
s=s[:a]+'''// A tile includes the two-cell concentration halo needed by the composed
// Laplacians. Both stencils are fused without a global potential buffer.
constexpr unsigned TileX = 32, TileY = 8, ZTile = 4;

__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double cv, double laplacian) {
    constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * laplacian;
}

__global__ void fusedStencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                             double* __restrict__ output,
                             unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    constexpr unsigned CX = TileX + 4, CY = TileY + 4, CZ = ZTile + 4;
    constexpr unsigned MX = TileX + 2, MY = TileY + 2, MZ = ZTile + 2;
    __shared__ double c[CZ * CY * CX];
    __shared__ double mu[MZ * MY * MX];
    const unsigned xBase = blockIdx.x * TileX;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t yBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileY;
             yBase < ny; yBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < CX * CY * CZ; k += blockDim.x) {
                const size_t xx = static_cast<size_t>(xBase) + k % CX;
                const size_t yy = yBase + k / CX % CY;
                const size_t zz = zBase + k / (CX * CY);
                const size_t x = xx < 2 ? 0 : min(xx - 2, size_t{nx - 1});
                const size_t y = yy < 2 ? 0 : min(yy - 2, size_t{ny - 1});
                const size_t z = zz < 2 ? 0 : min(zz - 2, size_t{nz - 1});
                c[k] = input[z * plane + y * nx + x];
            }
            __syncthreads();
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < MX * MY * MZ; k += blockDim.x) {
                // Clamp the potential's coordinate before evaluating its
                // concentration stencil, including replicated halo values.
                const unsigned x = min(max(k % MX + 1, xBase == 0 ? 2u : 0u), nx - xBase + 1);
                const unsigned y = static_cast<unsigned>(min(max(size_t{k / MX % MY + 1}, yBase == 0 ? size_t{2} : size_t{0}), size_t{ny} - yBase + 1));
                const unsigned z = static_cast<unsigned>(min(max(size_t{k / (MX * MY) + 1}, zBase == 0 ? size_t{2} : size_t{0}), size_t{nz} - zBase + 1));
                const unsigned i = (z * CY + y) * CX + x;
                const double cv = c[i];
                const double cxx = c[i + 1] + c[i - 1] - 2.0 * cv;
                const double cyy = c[i + CX] + c[i - CX] - 2.0 * cv;
                const double czz = c[i + CX * CY] + c[i - CX * CY] - 2.0 * cv;
                mu[k] = chemicalPotential(cv, cxx + cyy + czz);
            }
            __syncthreads();
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < TileX * TileY * ZTile; k += blockDim.x) {
                const unsigned x = k % TileX, y = k / TileX % TileY, z = k / (TileX * TileY);
                if (xBase + x < nx && yBase + y < ny && zBase + z < nz) {
                    const unsigned m = ((z + 1) * MY + y + 1) * MX + x + 1;
                    const double center = mu[m];
                    const double cxx = mu[m + 1] + mu[m - 1] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cyy = mu[m + MX] + mu[m - MX] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double czz = mu[m + MX * MY] + mu[m - MX * MY] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cv = c[((z + 2) * CY + y + 2) * CX + x + 2];
                    output[(zBase + z) * plane + (yBase + y) * nx + xBase + x] =
                        cv + 0.01 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
                }
            }
            __syncthreads();
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double*,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    fusedStencil<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}
