#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// A tile includes the two-cell concentration halo needed by the composed
// Laplacians. Both stencils are fused without a global potential buffer.
constexpr unsigned TileX = 32, TileY = 8, ZTile = 4;

__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double cv, double laplacian) {
    constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * laplacian;
}

__global__ void fusedStencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                             double* __restrict__ output,
                             unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    constexpr unsigned CX = TileX + 4, CY = TileY + 4, CZ = ZTile + 4;
    constexpr unsigned MX = TileX + 2, MY = TileY + 2, MZ = ZTile + 2;
    __shared__ double c[CZ * CY * CX];
    __shared__ double mu[MZ * MY * MX];
    const unsigned xBase = blockIdx.x * TileX;
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t yBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * TileY;
             yBase < ny; yBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * TileY) {
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < CX * CY * CZ; k += blockDim.x) {
                const size_t xx = static_cast<size_t>(xBase) + k % CX;
                const size_t yy = yBase + k / CX % CY;
                const size_t zz = zBase + k / (CX * CY);
                const size_t x = xx < 2 ? 0 : min(xx - 2, size_t{nx - 1});
                const size_t y = yy < 2 ? 0 : min(yy - 2, size_t{ny - 1});
                const size_t z = zz < 2 ? 0 : min(zz - 2, size_t{nz - 1});
                c[k] = input[z * plane + y * nx + x];
            }
            __syncthreads();
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < MX * MY * MZ; k += blockDim.x) {
                // Clamp the potential's coordinate before evaluating its
                // concentration stencil, including replicated halo values.
                const unsigned x = min(max(k % MX + 1, xBase == 0 ? 2u : 0u), nx - xBase + 1);
                const unsigned y = static_cast<unsigned>(min(max(size_t{k / MX % MY + 1}, yBase == 0 ? size_t{2} : size_t{0}), size_t{ny} - yBase + 1));
                const unsigned z = static_cast<unsigned>(min(max(size_t{k / (MX * MY) + 1}, zBase == 0 ? size_t{2} : size_t{0}), size_t{nz} - zBase + 1));
                const unsigned i = (z * CY + y) * CX + x;
                const double cv = c[i];
                const double cxx = c[i + 1] + c[i - 1] - 2.0 * cv;
                const double cyy = c[i + CX] + c[i - CX] - 2.0 * cv;
                const double czz = c[i + CX * CY] + c[i - CX * CY] - 2.0 * cv;
                mu[k] = chemicalPotential(cv, cxx + cyy + czz);
            }
            __syncthreads();
            for (unsigned k = threadIdx.x; k < TileX * TileY * ZTile; k += blockDim.x) {
                const unsigned x = k % TileX, y = k / TileX % TileY, z = k / (TileX * TileY);
                if (xBase + x < nx && yBase + y < ny && zBase + z < nz) {
                    const unsigned m = ((z + 1) * MY + y + 1) * MX + x + 1;
                    const double center = mu[m];
                    const double cxx = mu[m + 1] + mu[m - 1] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cyy = mu[m + MX] + mu[m - MX] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double czz = mu[m + MX * MY] + mu[m - MX * MY] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cv = c[((z + 2) * CY + y + 2) * CX + x + 2];
                    output[(zBase + z) * plane + (yBase + y) * nx + xBase + x] =
                        cv + 0.01 * (cxx + cyy + czz);
                }
            }
            __syncthreads();
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double*,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    fusedStencil<<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        const dim3 block(256);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + TileX - 1) / TileX),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + TileY - 1) / TileY)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + ZTile - 1) / ZTile)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p=Path('cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
a=s.index('// Each warp follows X')
b=s.index('bool validateResult',a)
s=s[:a]+'''// X neighbors are exchanged within a warp; Y neighbors use a shared plane.
// Sliding registers reuse Z neighbors while each block advances through a slab.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    extern __shared__ double tile[];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned xRaw = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tx;
    const unsigned x = min(xRaw, nx - 1);
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t yBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y;
             yBase < ny; yBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            const size_t y = min(yBase + ty, size_t{ny - 1});
            size_t i = zBase * plane + y * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            const unsigned count = static_cast<unsigned>(min(size_t{ZTile}, size_t{nz} - zBase));
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                tile[(ty + 1) * blockDim.x + tx] = center;
                if (ty == 0) {
                    tile[tx] = input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)];
                }
                if (ty + 1 == blockDim.y) {
                    tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)];
                }
                __syncthreads();
                double left = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                double right = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                if (tx == 0) left = input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)];
                if (tx + 1 == blockDim.x) right = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)];
                if (x == nx - 1) right = center;
                const double cxx = right + left - 2.0 * center;
                const double cyy = tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] + tile[ty * blockDim.x + tx] - 2.0 * center;
                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                if (xRaw < nx && yBase + ty < ny) {
                    if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                        output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                           (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                                    + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                                    - 0.5 * laplacian;
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                }
                previous = center;
                center = following;
                __syncthreads();
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t sharedBytes = block.x * (block.y + 2) * sizeof(double);
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

''' +s[b:]
p.write_text(s)

codex
Fusing both stencils into one shared-memory tile was slower on this GPU because the halo cells repeat much of the chemical-potential work. I’m measuring a version that keeps two kernels but shares neighboring values within each block and reuses Z-plane values in registers.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// X neighbors are exchanged within a warp; Y neighbors use a shared plane.
// Sliding registers reuse Z neighbors while each block advances through a slab.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

template <bool ChemicalPotential>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    extern __shared__ double tile[];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned xRaw = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tx;
    const unsigned x = min(xRaw, nx - 1);
    const size_t plane = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;
    for (size_t zBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.z) * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.z) * ZTile) {
        for (size_t yBase = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y;
             yBase < ny; yBase += static_cast<size_t>(gridDim.y) * blockDim.y) {
            const size_t y = min(yBase + ty, size_t{ny - 1});
            size_t i = zBase * plane + y * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            const unsigned count = static_cast<unsigned>(min(size_t{ZTile}, size_t{nz} - zBase));
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                tile[(ty + 1) * blockDim.x + tx] = center;
                if (ty == 0) {
                    tile[tx] = input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)];
                }
                if (ty + 1 == blockDim.y) {
                    tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)];
                }
                __syncthreads();
                double left = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                double right = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                if (tx == 0) left = input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)];
                if (tx + 1 == blockDim.x) right = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)];
                if (x == nx - 1) right = center;
                const double cxx = right + left - 2.0 * center;
                const double cyy = tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] + tile[ty * blockDim.x + tx] - 2.0 * center;
                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                if (xRaw < nx && yBase + ty < ny) {
                    if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                        output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                           (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                                    + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                                    - 0.5 * laplacian;
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                }
                previous = center;
                center = following;
                __syncthreads();
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t sharedBytes = block.x * (block.y + 2) * sizeof(double);
    stencil<true><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + ZTile - 1) / ZTile)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+            const unsigned count = static_cast<unsigned>(min(size_t{ZTile}, size_t{nz} - zBase));
+            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
+                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
+                const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +

+                const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
+                                   input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
+                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;

+            if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {

+                constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
+                constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
+                output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
+                                   (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
+                            + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
+                            - 0.5 * laplacian;
+            } else {

             }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>

#include <vector>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>

#include <cuda_runtime.h>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// X neighbors are exchanged within a warp; Y neighbors use a shared plane.
// Sliding registers reuse Z neighbors while each block advances through a slab.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

template <bool ChemicalPotential, typename Index>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    extern __shared__ double tile[];
    const unsigned tx = threadIdx.x, ty = threadIdx.y;
    const unsigned xRaw = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tx;
    const unsigned x = min(xRaw, nx - 1);
    const Index plane = static_cast<Index>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned zBase = blockIdx.z * ZTile;
         zBase < nz; zBase += gridDim.z * ZTile) {
        for (unsigned yBase = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y;
             yBase < ny; yBase += gridDim.y * blockDim.y) {
            const unsigned y = min(yBase + ty, ny - 1);
            Index i = static_cast<Index>(zBase) * plane + static_cast<Index>(y) * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            const unsigned count = min(ZTile, nz - zBase);
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < count; ++offset, i += plane) {
                const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                tile[(ty + 1) * blockDim.x + tx] = center;
                if (ty == 0) {
                    tile[tx] = input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)];
                }
                if (ty + 1 == blockDim.y) {
                    tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)];
                }
                __syncthreads();
                double left = __shfl_up_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                double right = __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff, center, 1, blockDim.x);
                if (tx == 0) left = input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)];
                if (tx + 1 == blockDim.x) right = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)];
                if (x == nx - 1) right = center;
                const double cxx = right + left - 2.0 * center;
                const double cyy = tile[(ty + 2) * blockDim.x + tx] + tile[ty * blockDim.x + tx] - 2.0 * center;
                const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                if (xRaw < nx && yBase + ty < ny) {
                    if constexpr (ChemicalPotential) {
                        constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
                        constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
                        output[i] = 4.5 * ((center + 1.0) * e_AA +
                                           (center - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * center * e_AB)
                                    + 3.0 * center + center * center * center
                                    - 0.5 * laplacian;
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                }
                previous = center;
                center = following;
                __syncthreads();
            }
        }
    }
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t sharedBytes = block.x * (block.y + 2) * sizeof(double);
    if (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny * nz <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        stencil<true, unsigned><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
        stencil<false, unsigned><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
    } else {
        stencil<true, size_t><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
        stencil<false, size_t><<<grid, block, sharedBytes, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + ZTile - 1) / ZTile)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

// previous and current planes at every cell. Small grids use one plane per
// block to expose enough independent work to the GPU.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double cv, double laplacian) {
    constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * laplacian;
}

template <bool Chemical, typename Index, unsigned Depth>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const Index plane = static_cast<Index>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned zBase = blockIdx.z * Depth;
         zBase < nz; zBase += gridDim.z * Depth) {
        for (unsigned y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += gridDim.y * blockDim.y) {
            Index i = static_cast<Index>(zBase) * plane + static_cast<Index>(y) * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < Depth; ++offset, i += plane) {
                if (zBase + offset < nz) {
                    const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                    const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                    const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                    if constexpr (Chemical) {
                        output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, laplacian);
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                    previous = center;
                    center = following;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

template <typename Index, unsigned Depth>
void launchStencils(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t volume = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny * nz;
    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        if (volume < (1u << 20)) {
            launchStencils<unsigned, 1>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        } else {
            launchStencils<unsigned, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        }
    } else {
        launchStencils<size_t, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
    }
}

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Coalesced XY planes and a register window along Z avoid reloading the
// previous and current planes at every cell. Small grids use one plane per
// block to expose enough independent work to the GPU.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double cv, double laplacian) {
    constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * laplacian;
}

template <bool Chemical, typename Index, unsigned Depth>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const Index plane = static_cast<Index>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned zBase = blockIdx.z * Depth;
         zBase < nz; zBase += gridDim.z * Depth) {
        for (unsigned y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += gridDim.y * blockDim.y) {
            Index i = static_cast<Index>(zBase) * plane + static_cast<Index>(y) * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < Depth; ++offset, i += plane) {
                if (zBase + offset < nz) {
                    const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                    const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                    const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                    if constexpr (Chemical) {
                        output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, laplacian);
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                    previous = center;
                    center = following;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

template <typename Index, unsigned Depth>
void launchStencils(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t volume = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny * nz;
    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        if (volume < (1u << 20)) {
            launchStencils<unsigned, 1>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        } else {
            launchStencils<unsigned, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        }
    } else {
        launchStencils<size_t, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const unsigned depth = gridSize < (1u << 20) ? 1 : ZTile;
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + depth - 1) / depth)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#include <algorithm>

// CUDA errors must not silently produce an incomplete result.
void checkCuda(cudaError_t status) {
    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
        throw std::runtime_error(cudaGetErrorString(status));
    }
}

class DeviceField {
public:
    explicit DeviceField(size_t bytes) { checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&data, bytes)); }
    ~DeviceField() { cudaFree(data); }
    DeviceField(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    DeviceField& operator=(const DeviceField&) = delete;
    double* data = nullptr;
};

class SimulationStream {
public:
    SimulationStream() { checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking)); }
    ~SimulationStream() { cudaStreamDestroy(stream); }
    SimulationStream(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    SimulationStream& operator=(const SimulationStream&) = delete;
    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
};

class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    ~TimeStepGraph() {
        if (executable) cudaGraphExecDestroy(executable);
        if (graph) cudaGraphDestroy(graph);
    }
    cudaGraph_t graph = nullptr;
    cudaGraphExec_t executable = nullptr;
};

__global__ void initializeConcentration(double* c, size_t volume) {
    for (size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume; i += static_cast<size_t>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x) {
        const double pseudo = (((i + 1) * size_t{1299709}) % volume) /
                              static_cast<double>(volume);
        c[i] = -1.0 + 2.0 * pseudo;
    }
}

// Coalesced XY planes and a register window along Z avoid reloading the
// previous and current planes at every cell. Small grids use one plane per
// block to expose enough independent work to the GPU.
constexpr unsigned ZTile = 8;

__device__ __forceinline__ double chemicalPotential(double cv, double laplacian) {
    constexpr double e_AA = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_BB = -(2.0 / 9.0);
    constexpr double e_AB = 2.0 / 9.0;
    return 4.5 * ((cv + 1.0) * e_AA + (cv - 1.0) * e_BB - 2.0 * cv * e_AB)
           + 3.0 * cv + cv * cv * cv - 0.5 * laplacian;
}

template <bool Chemical, typename Index, unsigned Depth>
__global__ void stencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                        const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                        double* __restrict__ output,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const unsigned x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (x >= nx) return;
    const Index plane = static_cast<Index>(nx) * ny;
    for (unsigned zBase = blockIdx.z * Depth;
         zBase < nz; zBase += gridDim.z * Depth) {
        for (unsigned y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
             y < ny; y += gridDim.y * blockDim.y) {
            Index i = static_cast<Index>(zBase) * plane + static_cast<Index>(y) * nx + x;
            double previous = input[i - (zBase > 0 ? plane : 0)];
            double center = input[i];
            #pragma unroll
            for (unsigned offset = 0; offset < Depth; ++offset, i += plane) {
                if (zBase + offset < nz) {
                    const double following = input[i + (zBase + offset + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)];
                    const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                                       input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
                    const double czz = following + previous - 2.0 * center;
                    const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
                    if constexpr (Chemical) {
                        output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, laplacian);
                    } else {
                        output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
                    }
                    previous = center;
                    center = following;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

template <typename Index, unsigned Depth>
void launchStencils(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    stencil<true, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    stencil<false, Index, Depth><<<grid, block, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

void launchTimeStep(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                    unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                    dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t volume = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny * nz;
    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        if (volume < (1u << 20)) {
            launchStencils<unsigned, 1>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        } else {
            launchStencils<unsigned, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        }
    } else {
        launchStencils<size_t, ZTile>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& c, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Check for NaN or Inf
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check if values are in reasonable range for concentration field
    // After Cahn-Hilliard evolution, values should typically remain bounded
    double minVal = c[0];
    double maxVal = c[0];
    for (const auto& val : c) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Concentration range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // Values should generally stay within reasonable bounds
    if (maxVal > 10.0 || minVal < -10.0) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 64)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of time steps (default: 20)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 64;
    size_t ny = 0;
    size_t nz = 0;
    int iterations = 20;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("Cahn-Hilliard Phase Separation Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Time steps: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    if (nx == 0 || nx > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        ny > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nz > std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max() ||
        nx > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / ny ||
        nx * ny > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / nz ||
        nx * ny * nz > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
        fprintf(stderr, "Invalid or overflowing grid dimensions\n");
        return 1;
    }
    const size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    std::vector<double> cold;

    try {
        DeviceField field0(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField field1(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        DeviceField potential(gridSize * sizeof(double));
        SimulationStream simulation;
        const cudaStream_t stream = simulation.stream;
        double* current = field0.data;
        double* next = field1.data;
        double* mu = potential.data;

        // Narrow grids still fill a block with useful rows. Limit Y/Z launch
        // dimensions and traverse any remaining slabs inside the kernel.
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 32) blockX *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, 256 / blockX);
        const unsigned depth = gridSize < (1u << 20) ? 1 : ZTile;
        const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned>((nx + block.x - 1) / block.x),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (ny + block.y - 1) / block.y)),
                        static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (nz + depth - 1) / depth)));

        printf("Initializing concentration field...\n");
        const unsigned initBlocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (gridSize + 255) / 256));
        initializeConcentration<<<initBlocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(current, gridSize);
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());

        // An even-sized graph restores buffer parity, so it can be replayed
        // for arbitrarily many steps with no per-step host synchronization.
        TimeStepGraph steps;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
            for (int t = 0; t < graphSteps; t += 2) {
                launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
                launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            }
            checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
            checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
            checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
        }
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));

        printf("Running Cahn-Hilliard simulation...\n");
        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
        int t = 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            for (; t <= iterations - graphSteps; t += graphSteps) {
                checkCuda(cudaGraphLaunch(steps.executable, stream));
            }
        }
        for (; t < iterations; ++t) {
            launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
            std::swap(current, next);
        }
        checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        const double milliseconds = std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - start).count();
        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        const double mcups = static_cast<double>(gridSize) * iterations / (milliseconds * 1000.0);
        printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);

        // Host transfers are needed only for the original reporting options.
        if (printResults || validate) {
            cold.resize(gridSize);
            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(cold.data(), current, gridSize * sizeof(double),
                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, stream));
            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));
        }
    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA simulation failed: %s\n", error.what());
        return 1;
    }

    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(cold, "Concentration");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(cold, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
Shared-memory staging gave little benefit over CUDA’s cache on the tested grids, so I’m keeping coalesced stencil loads and reuse of Z-plane values in registers. Large-grid throughput is about 7.7 billion cell updates per second. I’ll finish the layout tuning, then run the final reference comparisons and sanitizer checks.

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('cahn-hilliard/cahn_hilliard.cpp')
s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('class TimeStepGraph {\\npublic:\\n', '''class TimeStepGraph {
public:
    TimeStepGraph() = default;
    TimeStepGraph(const TimeStepGraph&) = delete;
    TimeStepGraph& operator=(const TimeStepGraph&) = delete;
''')
a=s.index('template <typename Index, unsigned Depth>')
s=s[:a]+'''// A linear launch keeps very thin grids from wasting most lanes in XY tiles.
template <bool Chemical, typename Index>
__global__ void thinStencil(const double* __restrict__ input,
                            const double* __restrict__ concentration,
                            double* __restrict__ output,
                            unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz) {
    const Index plane = static_cast<Index>(nx) * ny;
    const Index volume = plane * nz;
    const Index stride = static_cast<Index>(blockDim.x) * gridDim.x;
    for (Index i = static_cast<Index>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
         i < volume;) {
        const unsigned x = static_cast<unsigned>(i % nx);
        const unsigned y = static_cast<unsigned>(i / nx % ny);
        const unsigned z = static_cast<unsigned>(i / plane);
        const double center = input[i];
        const double cxx = input[i + (x + 1 < nx ? 1 : 0)] +
                           input[i - (x > 0 ? 1 : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
        const double cyy = input[i + (y + 1 < ny ? nx : 0)] +
                           input[i - (y > 0 ? nx : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
        const double czz = input[i + (z + 1 < nz ? plane : 0)] +
                           input[i - (z > 0 ? plane : 0)] - 2.0 * center;
        const double laplacian = cxx + cyy + czz;
        if constexpr (Chemical) {
            output[i] = chemicalPotential(center, laplacian);
        } else {
            output[i] = concentration[i] + 0.01 * laplacian;
        }
        // Avoid overflow on the last grid-stride increment.
        if (volume - i <= stride) break;
        i += stride;
    }
}

template <typename Index>
void launchThinStencils(double* cold, double* cnew, double* mu,
                        unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                        cudaStream_t stream) {
    const size_t volume = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny * nz;
    const unsigned blocks = static_cast<unsigned>(std::min<size_t>(65535, (volume + 255) / 256));
    thinStencil<true, Index><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(cold, nullptr, mu, nx, ny, nz);
    thinStencil<false, Index><<<blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(mu, cold, cnew, nx, ny, nz);
}

''' +s[a:]
s=s.replace('''    if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        if (volume < (1u << 20)) {''', '''    if (static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny < 256) {
        if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
            launchThinStencils<unsigned>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        } else {
            launchThinStencils<size_t>(cold, cnew, mu, nx, ny, nz, stream);
        }
    } else if (volume <= std::numeric_limits<unsigned>::max()) {
        if (volume < (1u << 20)) {''')
a=s.index('bool validateResult')
s=s[:a]+'''void captureTimeSteps(TimeStepGraph& steps, int count,
                      double* current, double* next, double* mu,
                      unsigned nx, unsigned ny, unsigned nz,
                      dim3 grid, dim3 block, cudaStream_t stream) {
    checkCuda(cudaStreamBeginCapture(stream, cudaStreamCaptureModeThreadLocal));
    for (int t = 0; t < count; t += 2) {
        launchTimeStep(current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        launchTimeStep(next, current, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
    }
    checkCuda(cudaGetLastError());
    checkCuda(cudaStreamEndCapture(stream, &steps.graph));
    checkCuda(cudaGraphInstantiate(&steps.executable, steps.graph, nullptr, nullptr, 0));
    checkCuda(cudaGraphUpload(steps.executable, stream));
}

''' +s[a:]
a=s.index('        unsigned blockX = 1;')
b=s.index('        const unsigned depth',a)
s=s[:a]+'''        unsigned minimumY = 1;
        while (minimumY < ny && minimumY < 8) minimumY *= 2;
        unsigned blockX = 1;
        while (blockX < nx && blockX < 256 / minimumY) blockX *= 2;
        unsigned blockY = 1;
        while (blockY < ny && blockY < 256 / blockX) blockY *= 2;
        const dim3 block(blockX, blockY);
''' +s[b:]
a=s.index('        TimeStepGraph steps;')
b=s.index('        checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));',a)
s=s[:a]+'''        TimeStepGraph steps, tail;
        const int graphSteps = std::min(32, iterations > 1 ? iterations - iterations % 2 : 0);
        const int tailSteps = graphSteps > 0 ? (iterations % graphSteps) / 2 * 2 : 0;
        if (graphSteps > 0) {
            captureTimeSteps(steps, graphSteps, current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        }
        if (tailSteps > 0) {
            captureTimeSteps(tail, tailSteps, current, next, mu, nx, ny, nz, grid, block, stream);
        }
''' +s[b:]

            t += tailSteps;
        }
''' +s[a:]
p.write_text(s)

#include <chrono>
